Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < j0 + jb; ++i) {\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return SIZE_MAX;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L11^T = A21 in place for the ib x jb panel block at (i0, j0),\n// where L11 is the already-factorized diagonal block at (j0, j0).\nstatic void trsmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ai = &A[i * n];\n        for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n            const double* Aj = &A[j * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += Ai[k] * Aj[k];\n            }\n            Ai[j] = (Ai[j] - sum) / Aj[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing update: C(i0,j0) -= A(i0,k0) * A(j0,k0)^T for an ib x jb block.\n// When i0 == j0 (diagonal block) only the lower triangle is updated,\n// matching what the unblocked algorithm ever reads or writes there.\nstatic void gemmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb,\n                      const size_t k0, const size_t kb) {\n    // Transpose the B panel block into a contiguous buffer so the innermost\n    // loop is a stride-1 SAXPY over j (vectorizes without FP reassociation).\n    double Bt[BS_MAX * BS_MAX];\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n        const double* Bj = &A[(j0 + j) * n + k0];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            Bt[k * jb + j] = Bj[k];\n        }\n    }\n    const bool diag = (i0 == j0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ci = &A[(i0 + i) * n + j0];\n        const double* Ai = &A[(i0 + i) * n + k0];\n        const size_t jend = diag ? (i + 1) : jb;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            const double a = Ai[k];\n            const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jend; ++j) {\n                Ci[j] -= a * btk[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Block size: large enough for cache-efficient kernels, small enough to\n    // expose plenty of tasks to the available threads.\n    const size_t bs = std::clamp<size_t>(((n / 32 + 7) / 8) * 8, 32, BS_MAX);\n    const size_t nb = (n + bs - 1) / bs; // number of block rows/columns\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite pivot (SIZE_MAX if none).\n    std::atomic<size_t> badIndex{SIZE_MAX};\n\n    auto bdim = [&](size_t b) { return std::min(bs, n - b * bs); };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    #pragma omp single nowait\n    {\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n            const size_t k0 = k * bs;\n            const size_t kb = bdim(k);\n\n            // Task dependencies use the top-left element of each block as anchor.\n            #pragma omp task depend(inout: a[k0 * n + k0]) firstprivate(k0, kb) shared(badIndex)\n            {\n                if (badIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == SIZE_MAX) {\n                    const size_t bad = potrfBlock(a, n, k0, kb);\n                    if (bad != SIZE_MAX) {\n                        size_t expected = SIZE_MAX;\n                        badIndex.compare_exchange_strong(expected, bad);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                const size_t i0 = i * bs;\n                const size_t ib = bdim(i);\n\n                #pragma omp task depend(in: a[k0 * n + k0]) depend(inout: a[i0 * n + k0]) \\\n                    firstprivate(i0, ib, k0, kb) shared(badIndex)\n                {\n                    if (badIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == SIZE_MAX) {\n                        trsmBlock(a, n, i0, ib, k0, kb);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                const size_t i0 = i * bs;\n                const size_t ib = bdim(i);\n                for (size_t j = k + 1; j <= i; ++j) {\n                    const size_t j0 = j * bs;\n                    const size_t jb = bdim(j);\n\n                    #pragma omp task depend(in: a[i0 * n + k0], a[j0 * n + k0]) \\\n                        depend(inout: a[i0 * n + j0]) \\\n                        firstprivate(i0, ib, j0, jb, k0, kb) shared(badIndex)\n                    {\n                        if (badIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == SIZE_MAX) {\n                            gemmBlock(a, n, i0, ib, j0, jb, k0, kb);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    } // implicit barrier: all tasks complete here\n\n    const size_t bad = badIndex.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n    if (bad != SIZE_MAX) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", bad);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            a[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max: maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; // Save original for validation\n    }\n    \n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":104,"oldLines":63,"newStart":104,"newLines":56,"lines":["     const size_t nb = (n + bs - 1) / bs; Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < j0 + jb; ++i) {\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return SIZE_MAX;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L11^T = A21 in place for the ib x jb panel block at (i0, j0),\n// where L11 is the already-factorized diagonal block at (j0, j0).\nstatic void trsmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ai = &A[i * n];\n        for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n            const double* Aj = &A[j * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += Ai[k] * Aj[k];\n            }\n            Ai[j] = (Ai[j] - sum) / Aj[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing update: C(i0,j0) -= A(i0,k0) * A(j0,k0)^T for an ib x jb block.\n// When i0 == j0 (diagonal block) only the lower triangle is updated,\n// matching what the unblocked algorithm ever reads or writes there.\nstatic void gemmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb,\n                      const size_t k0, const size_t kb) {\n    // Transpose the B panel block into a contiguous buffer so the innermost\n    // loop is a stride-1 SAXPY over j (vectorizes without FP reassociation).\n    double Bt[BS_MAX * BS_MAX];\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n        const double* Bj = &A[(j0 + j) * n + k0];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            Bt[k * jb + j] = Bj[k];\n        }\n    }\n    const bool diag = (i0 == j0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ci = &A[(i0 + i) * n + j0];\n        const double* Ai = &A[(i0 + i) * n + k0];\n        const size_t jend = diag ? j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < j0 + jb; ++i) {\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return SIZE_MAX;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L11^T = A21 in place for the ib x jb panel block at (i0, j0),\n// where L11 is the already-factorized diagonal block at (j0, j0).\nstatic void trsmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ai = &A[i * n];\n        for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n            const double* Aj = &A[j * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += Ai[k] * Aj[k];\n            }\n            Ai[j] = (Ai[j] - sum) / Aj[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing update: C(i0,j0) -= A(i0,k0) * A(j0,k0)^T for an ib x jb block.\n// When i0 == j0 (diagonal block) only the lower triangle is updated,\n// matching what the unblocked algorithm ever reads or writes there.\nstatic void gemmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb,\n                      const size_t k0, const size_t kb) {\n    // Transpose the B panel block into a contiguous buffer so the innermost\n    // loop is a stride-1 SAXPY over j (vectorizes without FP reassociation).\n    double Bt[BS_MAX * BS_MAX];\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n        const double* Bj = &A[(j0 + j) * n + k0];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            Bt[k * jb + j] = Bj[k];\n        }\n    }\n    const bool diag = (i0 == j0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ci = &A[(i0 + i) * n + j0];\n        const double* Ai = &A[(i0 + i) * n + k0];\n        const size_t jend = diag ? (i + 1) : jb;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            const double a = Ai[k];\n            const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jend; ++j) {\n                Ci[j] -= a * btk[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Block size: large enough for cache-efficient kernels, small enough to\n    // expose plenty of tasks to the available threads.\n    const size_t bs = std::clamp<size_t>(((n / 32 + 7) / 8) * 8, 32, BS_MAX);\n    const size_t nb = (n + bs - 1) / bs; // number of block rows/columns\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite pivot (SIZE_MAX if none).\n    size_t badIndex = SIZE_MAX;\n\n    auto bdim = [&](size_t b) { return std::min(bs, n - b * bs); };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel shared(badIndex)\n    {\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n            const size_t k0 = k * bs;\n            const size_t kb = bdim(k);\n\n            // Factorize the diagonal block on one thread.\n            #pragma omp single\n            badIndex = potrfBlock(a, n, k0, kb);\n            // (implicit barrier: all threads see badIndex and the factored block)\n\n            if (badIndex != SIZE_MAX) {\n                break; // uniform across threads\n            }\n\n            // Solve the panel below the diagonal block.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                trsmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: panel done before trailing update reads it)\n\n            // Rank-kb update of the trailing submatrix. Flatten the triangular\n            // set of blocks {(i, j) : k < j <= i < nb} into a 1-D iteration\n            // space so a single worksharing loop covers all of them.\n            const size_t rows = nb - (k + 1);\n            const size_t nBlocks = rows * (rows + 1) / 2;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < nBlocks; ++t) {\n                // Invert t = ri*(ri+1)/2 + cj with 0 <= cj <= ri.\n                size_t ri = (size_t)((sqrt(8.0 * (double)t + 1.0) - 1.0) / 2.0);\n                while (ri * (ri + 1) / 2 > t) --ri;\n                while ((ri + 1) * (ri + 2) / 2 <= t) ++ri;\n                const size_t cj = t - ri * (ri + 1) / 2;\n                const size_t i = k + 1 + ri;\n                const size_t j = k + 1 + cj;\n                gemmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), j * bs, bdim(j), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: block (k+1, k+1) fully updated before potrf(k+1))\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t bad = badIndex;\n    if (bad != SIZE_MAX) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", bad);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            a[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < j0 + jb; ++i) {\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return SIZE_MAX;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L11^T = A21 in place for the ib x jb panel block at (i0, j0),\n// where L11 is the already-factorized diagonal block at (j0, j0).\nstatic void trsmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ai = &A[i * n];\n        for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n            const double* Aj = &A[j * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += Ai[k] * Aj[k];\n            }\n            Ai[j] = (Ai[j] - sum) / Aj[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing update: C(i0,j0) -= A(i0,k0) * A(j0,k0)^T for an ib x jb block.\n// When i0 == j0 (diagonal block) only the lower triangle is updated,\n// matching what the unblocked algorithm ever reads or writes there.\nstatic void gemmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb,\n                      const size_t k0, const size_t kb) {\n    // Transpose the B panel block into a contiguous buffer so the innermost\n    // loop is a stride-1 SAXPY over j (vectorizes without FP reassociation).\n    double Bt[BS_MAX * BS_MAX];\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n        const double* Bj = &A[(j0 + j) * n + k0];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            Bt[k * jb + j] = Bj[k];\n        }\n    }\n    const bool diag = (i0 == j0);\n    size_t i = 0;\n    // Process 4 rows at a time so each Bt row loaded from cache is reused\n    // across 4 independent accumulator streams.\n    for (; !diag && i + 4 <= ib; i += 4) {\n        double* C0 = &A[(i0 + i + 0) * n + j0];\n        double* C1 = &A[(i0 + i + 1) * n + j0];\n        double* C2 = &A[(i0 + i + 2) * n + j0];\n        double* C3 = &A[(i0 + i + 3) * n + j0];\n        const double* A0 = &A[(i0 + i + 0) * n + k0];\n        const double* A1 = &A[(i0 + i + 1) * n + k0];\n        const double* A2 = &A[(i0 + i + 2) * n + k0];\n        const double* A3 = &A[(i0 + i + 3) * n + k0];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            const double a0 = A0[k], a1 = A1[k], a2 = A2[k], a3 = A3[k];\n            const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n                const double b = btk[j];\n                C0[j] -= a0 * b;\n                C1[j] -= a1 * b;\n                C2[j] -= a2 * b;\n                C3[j] -= a3 * b;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    for (; i < ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ci = &A[(i0 + i) * n + j0];\n        const double* Ai = &A[(i0 + i) * n + k0];\n        const size_t jend = diag ? (i + 1) : jb;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            const double a = Ai[k];\n            const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jend; ++j) {\n                Ci[j] -= a * btk[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Block size: large enough for cache-efficient kernels, small enough to\n    // expose plenty of tasks to the available threads.\n    const size_t bs = std::clamp<size_t>(((n / 32 + 7) / 8) * 8, 32, BS_MAX);\n    const size_t nb = (n + bs - 1) / bs; // number of block rows/columns\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite pivot (SIZE_MAX if none).\n    size_t badIndex = SIZE_MAX;\n\n    auto bdim = [&](size_t b) { return std::min(bs, n - b * bs); };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel shared(badIndex)\n    {\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n            const size_t k0 = k * bs;\n            const size_t kb = bdim(k);\n\n            // Factorize the diagonal block on one thread.\n            #pragma omp single\n            badIndex = potrfBlock(a, n, k0, kb);\n            // (implicit barrier: all threads see badIndex and the factored block)\n\n            if (badIndex != SIZE_MAX) {\n                break; // uniform across threads\n            }\n\n            // Solve the panel below the diagonal block.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                trsmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: panel done before trailing update reads it)\n\n            // Rank-kb update of the trailing submatrix. Flatten the triangular\n            // set of blocks {(i, j) : k < j <= i < nb} into a 1-D iteration\n            // space so a single worksharing loop covers all of them.\n            const size_t rows = nb - (k + 1);\n            const size_t nBlocks = rows * (rows + 1) / 2;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < nBlocks; ++t) {\n                // Invert t = ri*(ri+1)/2 + cj with 0 <= cj <= ri.\n                size_t ri = (size_t)((sqrt(8.0 * (double)t + 1.0) - 1.0) / 2.0);\n                while (ri * (ri + 1) / 2 > t) --ri;\n                while ((ri + 1) * (ri + 2) / 2 <= t) ++ri;\n                const size_t cj = t - ri * (ri + 1) / 2;\n                const size_t i = k + 1 + ri;\n                const size_t j = k + 1 + cj;\n                gemmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), j * bs, bdim(j), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: block (k+1, k+1) fully updated before potrf(k+1))\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t bad = badIndex;\n    if (bad != SIZE_MAX) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", bad);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            a[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max: maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n// SMT/hyperthread siblings only contend for the FP units in this workload.\n// Unless the user pinned a thread count via OMP_NUM_THREADS, default to one\n// OpenMP thread per physical core.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n    int threadsPerCore = 1;\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (f) {\n        char buf[256];\n        if (fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f)) {\n            // Formats like \"0,128\" or \"0-1\": count of sibling CPUs listed.\n            int count = 0;\n            for (char* tok = strtok(buf, \",\"); tok; tok = strtok(nullptr, \",\")) {\n                int lo, hi;\n                if (sscanf(tok, \"%d-%d\", &lo, &hi) == 2) {\n                    count += hi - lo + 1;\n                } else {\n                    count += 1;\n                }\n            }\n            if (count > 1) {\n                threadsPerCore = count;\n            }\n        }\n        fclose(f);\n    }\n    const int physCores = std::max(1, omp_get_num_procs() / threadsPerCore);\n    omp_set_num_threads(physCores);\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < j0 + jb; ++i) {\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return SIZE_MAX;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L11^T = A21 in place for the ib x jb panel block at (i0, j0),\n// where L11 is the already-factorized diagonal block at (j0, j0).\nstatic void trsmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t i = i0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += Ai[k] * Aj[k];\n            }\n            Ai[j] = (Ai[j] - sum) / Aj[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing update: C(i0,j0) -= A(i0,k0) * A(j0,k0)^T for an ib x jb block.\n// When i0 == j0 (diagonal block) only the lower triangle is updated,\n// matching what the unblocked algorithm ever reads or writes there.\nstatic void gemmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb,\n                      const size_t k0, const size_t kb) {\n    // Transpose the B panel block into a contiguous buffer so the innermost\n    // loop is a stride-1 SAXPY over j (vectorizes without FP reassociation).\n    double Bt[BS_MAX * BS_MAX];\n    for (size_t j = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            const double a0 = A0[k], a1 = A1[k], a2 = A2[k], a3 = A3[k];\n            const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n                const double b = btk[j];\n                C0[j] -= a0 * b;\n                C1[j] -= a1 * b;\n                C2[j] -= a2 * b;\n                C3[j] -= a3 * b;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    for (; i < ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ci = &A[(i0 + i) * n + j0];\n        const double* Ai = &A[(i0 + i) * n + k0];\n        const size_t jend = diag ? (i + 1) : jb;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            const double a = Ai[k];\n            const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jend; ++j) {\n                Ci[j] -= a * btk[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Block size: large enough for cache-efficient kernels, small enough to\n    // expose plenty of tasks to the available threads.\n    const size_t bs = std::clamp<size_t>(((n / 32 + 7) / 8) * 8, 32, BS_MAX);\n    const size_t nb = (n + bs - 1) / bs; // number of block rows/columns\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite pivot (SIZE_MAX if none).\n    size_t badIndex = SIZE_MAX;\n\n    auto bdim = [&](size_t b) { return std::min(bs, n - b * bs); };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel shared(badIndex)\n    {\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n            const size_t k0 = k * bs;\n            const size_t kb = bdim(k);\n\n            // Factorize the diagonal block on one thread.\n            #pragma omp single\n            badIndex = potrfBlock(a, n, k0, kb);\n            // (implicit barrier: all threads see badIndex and the factored block)\n\n            if (badIndex != SIZE_MAX) {\n                break; // uniform across threads\n            }\n\n            // Solve the panel below the diagonal block.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                trsmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: panel done before trailing update reads it)\n\n            // Rank-kb update of the trailing submatrix. Flatten the triangular\n            // set of blocks {(i, j) : k < j <= i < nb} into a 1-D iteration\n            // space so a single worksharing loop covers all of them.\n            const size_t rows = nb - (k + 1);\n            const size_t nBlocks = rows * (rows + 1) / 2;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < nBlocks; ++t) {\n                // Invert t = ri*(ri+1)/2 + cj with 0 <= cj <= ri.\n                size_t ri = (size_t)((sqrt(8.0 * (double)t + 1.0) - 1.0) / 2.0);\n                while (ri * (ri + 1) / 2 > t) --ri;\n                while ((ri + 1) * (ri + 2) / 2 <= t) ++ri;\n                const size_t cj = t - ri * (ri + 1) / 2;\n                const size_t i = k + 1 + ri;\n                const size_t j = k + 1 + cj;\n                gemmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), j * bs, bdim(j), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: block (k+1, k+1) fully updated before potrf(k+1))\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t bad = badIndex;\n    if (bad != SIZE_MAX) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", bad);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            a[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max: maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n// SMT/hyperthread siblings only contend for the FP units in this workload.\n// Unless the user pinned a thread count via OMP_NUM_THREADS, default to one\n// OpenMP thread per physical core.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n    int threadsPerCore = 1;\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (f) {\n        char buf[256];\n        if (fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f)) {\n            // Formats like \"0,128\" or \"0-1\": count of sibling CPUs listed.\n            int count = 0;\n            for (char* tok = strtok(buf, \",\"); tok; tok = strtok(nullptr, \",\")) {\n                int lo, hi;\n                if (sscanf(tok, \"%d-%d\", &lo, &hi) == 2) {\n                    count += hi - lo + 1;\n                } else {\n                    count += 1;\n                }\n            }\n            if (count > 1) {\n                threadsPerCore = count;\n            }\n        }\n        fclose(f);\n    }\n    const int physCores = std::max(1, omp_get_num_procs() / threadsPerCore);\n    omp_set_num_threads(physCores);\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    configureThreads();\n\n    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    \n    // Allocate matrix\n    std::vector<double> A(n * n);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n    \n    // Generate positive definite matrix\n    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);\n    \n    if (validate) {\n        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation\n    }\n    \n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":81,"oldLines":31,"newStart":81,"newLines":7,"lines":["         }","     }","     const bool diag = (i0 == j0);","-    size_t i = 0;","-    // Process 4 rows at a time so each Bt row loaded from cache is reused","-    // across 4 independent accumulator streams.","-    for (; Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < j0 + jb; ++i) {\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return SIZE_MAX;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L11^T = A21 in place for the ib x jb panel block at (i0, j0),\n// where L11 is the already-factorized diagonal block at (j0, j0).\nstatic void trsmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ai = &A[i * n];\n        for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n            const double* Aj = &A[j * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += Ai[k] * Aj[k];\n            }\n            Ai[j] = (Ai[j] - sum) / Aj[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing update: C(i0,j0) -= A(i0,k0) * A(j0,k0)^T for an ib x jb block.\n// When i0 == j0 (diagonal block) only the lower triangle is updated,\n// matching what the unblocked algorithm ever reads or writes there.\nstatic void gemmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb,\n                      const size_t k0, const size_t kb) {\n    // Transpose the B panel block into a contiguous buffer so the innermost\n    // loop is a stride-1 SAXPY over j (vectorizes without FP reassociation).\n    double Bt[BS_MAX * BS_MAX];\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n        const double* Bj = &A[(j0 + j) * n + k0];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            Bt[k * jb + j] = Bj[k];\n        }\n    }\n    const bool diag = (i0 == j0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ci = &A[(i0 + i) * n + j0];\n        const double* Ai = &A[(i0 + i) * n + k0];\n        const size_t jend = diag ? (i + 1) : jb;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            const double a = Ai[k];\n            const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jend; ++j) {\n                Ci[j] -= a * btk[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Block size: large enough for cache-efficient kernels, small enough to\n    // expose plenty of tasks to the available threads.\n    const size_t bs = std::clamp<size_t>(((n / 32 + 7) / 8) * 8, 32, BS_MAX);\n    const size_t nb = (n + bs - 1) / bs; // number of block rows/columns\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite pivot (SIZE_MAX if none).\n    size_t badIndex = SIZE_MAX;\n\n    auto bdim = [&](size_t b) { return std::min(bs, n - b * bs); };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel shared(badIndex)\n    {\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n            const size_t k0 = k * bs;\n            const size_t kb = bdim(k);\n\n            // Factorize the diagonal block on one thread.\n            #pragma omp single\n            badIndex = potrfBlock(a, n, k0, kb);\n            // (implicit barrier: all threads see badIndex and the factored block)\n\n            if (badIndex != SIZE_MAX) {\n                break; // uniform across threads\n            }\n\n            // Solve the panel below the diagonal block.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                trsmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: panel done before trailing update reads it)\n\n            // Rank-kb update of the trailing submatrix. Flatten the triangular\n            // set of blocks {(i, j) : k < j <= i < nb} into a 1-D iteration\n            // space so a single worksharing loop covers all of them.\n            const size_t rows = nb - (k + 1);\n            const size_t nBlocks = rows * (rows + 1) / 2;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < nBlocks; ++t) {\n                // Invert t = ri*(ri+1)/2 + cj with 0 <= cj <= ri.\n                size_t ri = (size_t)((sqrt(8.0 * (double)t + 1.0) - 1.0) / 2.0);\n                while (ri * (ri + 1) / 2 > t) --ri;\n                while ((ri + 1) * (ri + 2) / 2 <= t) ++ri;\n                const size_t cj = t - ri * (ri + 1) / 2;\n                const size_t i = k + 1 + ri;\n                const size_t j = k + 1 + cj;\n                gemmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), j * bs, bdim(j), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: block (k+1, k+1) fully updated before potrf(k+1))\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t bad = badIndex;\n    if (bad != SIZE_MAX) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", bad);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            a[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max: maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n// SMT/hyperthread siblings only contend for the FP units in this workload.\n// Unless the user pinned a thread count via OMP_NUM_THREADS, default to one\n// OpenMP thread per physical core.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n    int threadsPerCore = 1;\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (f) {\n        char buf[256];\n        if (fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f)) {\n            // Formats like \"0,128\" or \"0-1\": count of sibling CPUs listed.\n            int count = 0;\n            for (char* tok = strtok(buf, \",\"); tok; tok = strtok(nullptr, \",\")) {\n                int lo, hi;\n                if (sscanf(tok, \"%d-%d\", &lo, &hi) == 2) {\n                    count += hi - lo + 1;\n                } else {\n                    count += 1;\n                }\n            }\n            if (count > 1) {\n                threadsPerCore = count;\n            }\n        }\n        fclose(f);\n    }\n    const int physCores = std::max(1, omp_get_num_procs() / threadsPerCore);\n    omp_set_num_threads(physCores);\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; Task dependencies on block anchors expose the full DAG parallelism.\n\nstatic constexpr size_t BS_MAX = 128;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky of the jb x jb diagonal block starting at (j0, j0).\n// Returns the global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element,\n// or SIZE_MAX on success.\nstatic size_t potrfBlock(double* A, const size_t n, const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n        }\n        const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        const double dj = sqrt(val);\n        A[j * n + j] = dj;\n        const double inv = 1.0 / dj;\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < j0 + jb; ++i) {\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return SIZE_MAX;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L11^T = A21 in place for the ib x jb panel block at (i0, j0),\n// where L11 is the already-factorized diagonal block at (j0, j0).\nstatic void trsmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb) {\n    for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ai = &A[i * n];\n        for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + jb; ++j) {\n            const double* Aj = &A[j * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = j0; k < j; ++k) {\n                sum += Ai[k] * Aj[k];\n            }\n            Ai[j] = (Ai[j] - sum) / Aj[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing update: C(i0,j0) -= A(i0,k0) * A(j0,k0)^T for an ib x jb block.\n// When i0 == j0 (diagonal block) only the lower triangle is updated,\n// matching what the unblocked algorithm ever reads or writes there.\nstatic void gemmBlock(double* A, const size_t n,\n                      const size_t i0, const size_t ib,\n                      const size_t j0, const size_t jb,\n                      const size_t k0, const size_t kb) {\n    // Transpose the B panel block into a contiguous buffer so the innermost\n    // loop is a stride-1 SAXPY over j (vectorizes without FP reassociation).\n    double Bt[BS_MAX * BS_MAX];\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n        const double* Bj = &A[(j0 + j) * n + k0];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            Bt[k * jb + j] = Bj[k];\n        }\n    }\n    const bool diag = (i0 == j0);\n\n    // Register-blocked micro-kernel: accumulate an MR x NR tile of C in\n    // registers across the whole k range, touching memory for C only once.\n    constexpr size_t MR = 4;\n    constexpr size_t NR = 8;\n    const size_t iMain = diag ? 0 : (ib / MR) * MR; // diagonal blocks use the generic path\n    const size_t jMain = (jb / NR) * NR;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < iMain; i += MR) {\n        const double* Arows[MR];\n        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < MR; ++ii) {\n            Arows[ii] = &A[(i0 + i + ii) * n + k0];\n        }\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < jMain; j += NR) {\n            double acc[MR][NR];\n            for (size_t ii = 0; ii < MR; ++ii) {\n                for (size_t jj = 0; jj < NR; ++jj) {\n                    acc[ii][jj] = A[(i0 + i + ii) * n + j0 + j + jj];\n                }\n            }\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n                const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb + j];\n                for (size_t ii = 0; ii < MR; ++ii) {\n                    const double a = Arows[ii][k];\n                    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < NR; ++jj) {\n                        acc[ii][jj] -= a * btk[jj];\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            for (size_t ii = 0; ii < MR; ++ii) {\n                for (size_t jj = 0; jj < NR; ++jj) {\n                    A[(i0 + i + ii) * n + j0 + j + jj] = acc[ii][jj];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        // Column remainder of the MR-row band.\n        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < MR; ++ii) {\n            double* Ci = &A[(i0 + i + ii) * n + j0];\n            const double* Ai = Arows[ii];\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n                const double a = Ai[k];\n                const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n                for (size_t j = jMain; j < jb; ++j) {\n                    Ci[j] -= a * btk[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Generic path: remaining rows, and entire diagonal blocks (lower triangle only).\n    for (size_t i = iMain; i < ib; ++i) {\n        double* Ci = &A[(i0 + i) * n + j0];\n        const double* Ai = &A[(i0 + i) * n + k0];\n        const size_t jend = diag ? (i + 1) : jb;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n            const double a = Ai[k];\n            const double* btk = &Bt[k * jb];\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jend; ++j) {\n                Ci[j] -= a * btk[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Block size: large enough for cache-efficient kernels, small enough to\n    // expose plenty of tasks to the available threads.\n    const size_t bs = std::clamp<size_t>(((n / 32 + 7) / 8) * 8, 32, BS_MAX);\n    const size_t nb = (n + bs - 1) / bs; // number of block rows/columns\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite pivot (SIZE_MAX if none).\n    size_t badIndex = SIZE_MAX;\n\n    auto bdim = [&](size_t b) { return std::min(bs, n - b * bs); };\n\n    #pragma omp parallel shared(badIndex)\n    {\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n            const size_t k0 = k * bs;\n            const size_t kb = bdim(k);\n\n            // Factorize the diagonal block on one thread.\n            #pragma omp single\n            badIndex = potrfBlock(a, n, k0, kb);\n            // (implicit barrier: all threads see badIndex and the factored block)\n\n            if (badIndex != SIZE_MAX) {\n                break; // uniform across threads\n            }\n\n            // Solve the panel below the diagonal block.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                trsmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: panel done before trailing update reads it)\n\n            // Rank-kb update of the trailing submatrix. Flatten the triangular\n            // set of blocks {(i, j) : k < j <= i < nb} into a 1-D iteration\n            // space so a single worksharing loop covers all of them.\n            const size_t rows = nb - (k + 1);\n            const size_t nBlocks = rows * (rows + 1) / 2;\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < nBlocks; ++t) {\n                // Invert t = ri*(ri+1)/2 + cj with 0 <= cj <= ri.\n                size_t ri = (size_t)((sqrt(8.0 * (double)t + 1.0) - 1.0) / 2.0);\n                while (ri * (ri + 1) / 2 > t) --ri;\n                while ((ri + 1) * (ri + 2) / 2 <= t) ++ri;\n                const size_t cj = t - ri * (ri + 1) / 2;\n                const size_t i = k + 1 + ri;\n                const size_t j = k + 1 + cj;\n                gemmBlock(a, n, i * bs, bdim(i), j * bs, bdim(j), k0, kb);\n            }\n            // (implicit barrier: block (k+1, k+1) fully updated before potrf(k+1))\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t bad = badIndex;\n    if (bad != SIZE_MAX) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", bad);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            a[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max: maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n// SMT/hyperthread siblings only contend for the FP units in this workload.\n// Unless the user pinned a thread count via OMP_NUM_THREADS, default to one\n// OpenMP thread per physical core.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n    int threadsPerCore = 1;\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (f) {\n        char buf[256];\n        if (fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f)) {\n            // Formats like \"0,128\" or \"0-1\": count of sibling CPUs listed.\n            int count = 0;\n            for (char* tok = strtok(buf, \",\"); tok; tok = strtok(nullptr, \",\")) {\n                int lo, hi;\n                if (sscanf(tok, \"%d-%d\", &lo, &hi) == 2) {\n                    count += hi - lo + 1;\n                } else {\n                    count += 1;\n                }\n            }\n            if (count > 1) {\n                threadsPerCore = count;\n            }\n        }\n        fclose(f);\n    }\n    const int physCores = std::max(1, omp_get_num_procs() / threadsPerCore);\n    omp_set_num_threads(physCores);\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    configureThreads();\n\n    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    \n    // Allocate matrix\n    std::vector<double> A(n * n);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n    \n    // Generate positive definite matrix\n    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);\n    \n    if (validate) {\n        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation\n    }\n    \n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":150,"oldLines":7,"newStart":150,"newLines":8,"lines":[" ","     // Block size: large enough for cache-efficient kernels, small enough to","     // expose plenty of tasks to the available threads.","-    const size_t bs = std::clamp<size_t>(((n / 32 + 7) / 8) * 8, 32, BS_MAX);","+    size_t bs = std::clamp<size_t>(((n / 32 + 7) / 8) * 8, 32, BS_MAX);","+    if (const char* e = getenv(\"CHOL_BS\")) bs = atoi(e);
